# Sequence Code-Gen — Reuse Plan & Unified UX Framing

Companion to `.megaplan/sequence-code-pipeline-feasibility.md`. That doc
audits whether the effects pipeline can be ported to sequences (verdict:
yes, ~70% reuse, 3.5–4 day MVP). This doc answers two follow-ups:

1. The user wants **one unified UX** where the agent routes between
   JSON-edit and code-edit — not two separate modes.
2. **What can we actually reuse vs. generalize vs. fork** from the
   effects pipeline at the code level.

Citations are `path:line` against the working tree at
`the project root`.

---

## 1. The unified-UX framing

The feasibility doc proposes a "Generate code" sub-modal alongside the
existing JSON-only `SequenceCreator`. The user's framing is stronger:
**single prompt box, agent decides path**. That requires three things
the existing audit doesn't cover.

### 1a. Three states a sequence-under-edit can be in

| State | Where source lives | What's editable cheaply |
|---|---|---|
| Theme-bundled | `vendor/timeline-theme-2rp/...` (build-time TSX) | Params only — code edits require redeploy or **forking to DB** |
| DB-code (new `type='sequence-component'`) | `resources.metadata.code` | Both params and code |
| JSON-only draft (current) | React state → timeline JSON on insert | Params only — there is no "code" yet |

The router has to know which state the selected clip is in *before* it
picks a path. The "fork to DB" transition (cloning a bundled component
into an editable DB resource) is the load-bearing UX moment that
doesn't exist today and isn't called out in the feasibility audit.

### 1b. Three request shapes the classifier should distinguish

- **Param-tweakable** — "slower", "use these images", "switch to
  vertical layout" *if* `layout` is already a schema field. → JSON path
  (cheap, retains `workerRender:true`).
- **Structurally-impossible-in-JSON** — "make the metric pulse on
  entry", "add a vignette behind the title", "use a custom easing curve
  mid-animation". → Code path (and if source is theme-bundled, fork-to-DB
  first).
- **Ambiguous** — "make it more dramatic". Cheapest move: try JSON
  first, offer "go deeper with code" as a follow-up.

A small classifier prompt (or a structured-output preamble on the
existing call) is enough — not a separate model. The classifier sees:
current schema, current params, user prompt → returns
`{path: 'json'|'code', reason}`. If `code` and source is theme-bundled,
the next step is the fork.

### 1c. What unifies in the UI

- Single prompt box, single Generate button.
- Side panel shows the same schema-driven editor (`SequenceParamEditor`)
  whether the user came in via JSON or code.
- A subtle badge on the result tells the user which path ran ("Edited
  params" vs "Generated component code"). Don't hide this — the
  worker-render capability flips between them and people will care
  later.
- "Edit" follow-ups can switch paths freely: a code-path result can
  still take a JSON-path follow-up if the next prompt only touches a
  known param.

### 1d. Risks specific to the unified flow

- **Worker-render asymmetry is a product issue, not a footnote.** JSON
  drafts route to `workerRender:true`; DB-code sequences must default
  `false` until the worker can compile. If a creator builds a 3-min
  film around a generated sequence and then can't render it
  server-side, that's a footgun. Surface the badge from day 1.
- **The "fork to DB" moment is the highest-friction UX in the unified
  flow.** A theme-bundled sequence is shared across the team's themes;
  cloning it into a per-user DB resource is correct but should feel
  like a deliberate user choice ("Customize this sequence for
  yourself"), not a silent drop-off.
- **Schema-vs-code drift is worse here.** If the agent flips to code
  path on Tuesday and JSON path on Wednesday for "the same" sequence,
  schemas can desync. The AST param-coverage check (feasibility doc §4
  item 1) is even more load-bearing in a unified UX than in two
  separate modes.

---

## 2. Reuse map — what to copy, generalize, or fork

After reading the effects pipeline end-to-end, the reuse picture is
clearer than "70%". The right answer is: copy some bits verbatim,
factor exactly three small abstractions, and explicitly *don't*
generalize others.

### Bucket 1 — copy-and-rename (don't try to be clever)

These are small enough that a shared abstraction would be more code
than two near-duplicates.

- **Edge-function skeleton.** `supabase/functions/ai-generate-effect/index.ts`
  is ~290 LOC of boilerplate (auth, rate-limit, Anthropic streaming,
  retry self-invoke at depth 1, structured response). Clone to
  `ai-generate-sequence-component/`. The streaming/SSE parsing
  (`callAnthropic`, lines 40–114) and retry self-invoke block (228–263)
  would be identical. Domain glue is just the
  `extractEffectCodeAndMeta`/`validateExtractedEffectCode` calls.
- **Edit-mode protocol.** `existingCode` round-trip
  (panel → edge → `templates.ts:159–171`). For sequences the field
  becomes `existingComponent` carrying `{code, schema, defaults}` but
  the shape and prompt-injection point are the same.
- **CreatorPanel UX skeleton.** Modal + Generate/Edit toggle + prompt
  textarea + abort controller + draft state + preview pane.
  `EffectCreatorPanel` and `SequenceCreatorPanel` are already cousins.
  Don't write `<CreatorPanel kind="effect|sequence">` — action buttons,
  asset wiring, and validation surfaces diverge enough that a generic
  shell will accumulate `if (kind === ...)` quickly.

### Bucket 2 — actually worth generalizing

These have a clean seam and you'll regret duplicating them.

- **`DynamicEffectRegistry` → `DynamicComponentRegistry<TProps>`.**
  The class (`DynamicEffectRegistry.ts`, 120 LOC) is already 90%
  domain-agnostic: subscribe/getSnapshot/version-bumping, `register`
  vs `registerAsync` with stale-pending guarding (lines 55–66),
  `batch()` for coalesced notifications, normalize-name for the
  `custom:` prefix. The only effect-specific thing is the
  `compileEffect`/`compileEffectAsync` import. Parameterize it:

  ```ts
  new DynamicComponentRegistry({
    builtIn,
    compile: (code) => tryCompileEffect(code),
    compileAsync: (code) => tryCompileEffectAsync(code),
  })
  ```

  Then `DynamicEffectRegistry` and `DynamicSequenceRegistry` become
  5-line factories.

- **The Sucrase + `new Function` compile harness.**
  `compileEffect.tsx:31–86` is one function with a hardcoded globals
  tuple. Lift to:

  ```ts
  function compileWithGlobals<TProps>(
    code: string,
    globals: Record<string, unknown>,
  ): CompileResult<FC<TProps>>
  ```

  Effects pass 8 globals; sequences pass ~14. Same Sucrase init, same
  IIFE wrapper, same `exports.default || module.exports.default ||
  module.exports` pickoff. The lazy-loaded `transformSync` cache
  (`compileEffect.tsx:6`) belongs in the shared module so Sucrase
  doesn't load twice.

- **Resources storage layer for code blobs.** `EffectMetadata`
  (`useResources.ts:80`) + `useListResources('effect', …)` + the
  create/update/delete trio is the *only* infra needed for sequence
  components. Two minimal changes:
  1. Add `'sequence-component'` to the `ResourceType` union
     (`useResources.ts:94`) and `isResourceType` (114).
  2. Define `SequenceComponentMetadata` mirroring `EffectMetadata`
     but with `{code, schemaJson, defaultsJson, clipType, themeId}`.

  No new table, no new RPC, no new RLS policy. `is_public` already
  exists (`20251207…` migration).

- **Prompt-output extraction harness.** `extractEffectCodeAndMeta`
  (`templates.ts:417–443`) implements a `// NAME / DESCRIPTION /
  PARAMS / MESSAGE` envelope around a code blob, with
  `stripMarkdownFences` + `stripRanges`. The envelope pattern is
  reusable; fields differ. Factor:

  ```ts
  parseEnvelope(text, ['NAME', 'DESCRIPTION', 'PARAMS', 'SCHEMA',
                       'DEFAULTS', 'MESSAGE'])
  ```

  Sequences need `SCHEMA` and `DEFAULTS` extra; effects don't use
  them. Auto-typo-fix (`templates.ts:425–432`) and `KNOWN_TYPOS`
  (449) are domain-specific — keep separate.

- **Retry-on-validation-failure self-invoke.** `index.ts:228–263` is a
  generic "edge function calls itself with `_retryDepth + 1` and the
  broken output as `_retryFailedCode`" pattern. Worth lifting to
  `_shared/aiCodegenRetry.ts` — otherwise the third codegen function
  will be a third copy.

### Bucket 3 — do NOT try to share

- **The system prompt.** `templates.ts`'s
  `EFFECT_COMPONENT_CONTRACT`/`AVAILABLE_GLOBALS`/`OUTPUT_RULES`/
  `VALIDATION_RULES` is a prompt-engineering artifact specific to
  entrance/exit effects and `React.createElement`-only output.
  Sequences need JSX-OK, theme-api globals, and a different model of
  what "a component" is. Sharing the prompt will produce worse output
  for both.
- **`validateExtractedEffectCode` regex set**
  (`templates.ts:458–492`). Encodes effect rules: `exports.default`,
  no imports, *requires* `React.createElement`, no `Math.random`
  outside `useMemo`, typo dictionary. Sequences want most of these
  except "requires React.createElement" (JSX is wanted) and need
  additional rules (param-coverage check, no `Date.now()`/
  `performance.now`, asset-key contract). Different rule set wearing
  the same shape — fork, or generalize as a `{rule, message}[]` and
  pass different lists.
- **`ParameterSchema` vs JSON Schema.** Effects use the custom
  `ParameterSchema` from `@/tools/video-editor/types`; sequences use
  JSON Schema (`vendor/.../schema.json`). Don't merge in MVP — let
  sequences keep JSON Schema and bridge to `SequenceParamEditor`'s
  existing reader.
- **Capability registry.** `SEQUENCE_CLIP_CAPABILITY_REGISTRY` is
  sequences-only (browserRender/workerRender/externalRender).
  Effects don't have an analogue. Keep it on the sequences side and
  force `workerRender:false` for DB-stored sequences via a
  capability builder, not via the shared registry.

### The minimum shared API to actually write

Three small modules, ~200 LOC total:

```
src/tools/video-editor/runtime-components/
  compileWithGlobals.ts          // Sucrase + new Function harness, generic
  DynamicComponentRegistry.ts    // generalized DynamicEffectRegistry
  index.ts                       // re-exports

supabase/functions/_shared/
  aiCodegenRetry.ts              // self-invoke retry helper
  promptEnvelope.ts              // parse // NAME / ... envelope, fences
```

Then:
- `DynamicEffectRegistry` becomes a 10-line factory wrapper around
  `DynamicComponentRegistry`.
- `DynamicSequenceRegistry` is the same wrapper with different globals.
- `ai-generate-effect/index.ts` shrinks ~80 LOC; the new
  `ai-generate-sequence-component/index.ts` lands at ~150 LOC instead
  of 290.

Everything else (panels, prompts, validators, schemas) stays forked.
The temptation is to generalize the prompt and validator because they
"look similar" — that's the trap. Generic surfaces are the bits that
touch *the runtime* (compilation, registration, storage), not the bits
that touch *the model* (prompts, parsing, rules).

---

## 3. Recommended sequencing

Same as the feasibility doc's D1 AM, with the unified-UX work folded
in after the runtime spike proves out:

1. **D1 AM — Contract spike (unchanged from audit).** Hand-port
   `ImageJumpSequence.tsx` to the "no imports, ~14 globals, JSX OK"
   form, prove Sucrase + `new Function` + injected theme-api globals
   render it as a custom `clipType` in the timeline. Catch impedance
   mismatches before touching the LLM. Until this works, the
   router/UX design is paper.
2. **D1 PM — Extract the three shared modules** (Bucket 2 above)
   while the spike is fresh. Refactor `DynamicEffectRegistry` to use
   `DynamicComponentRegistry` and confirm effects still work.
3. **D2 AM — `ai-generate-sequence-component` edge function.** Clone
   `ai-generate-effect/index.ts`, swap in sequence prompts (theme-api
   globals, ImageJump-style example), keep retry. Use the new
   `_shared/aiCodegenRetry.ts` and `_shared/promptEnvelope.ts`.
4. **D2 PM — Validators.** AST-walk param-coverage check (every
   `params.X` access is in `schema.properties` and `defaults`); extend
   determinism check to forbid `Date.now`/`performance.now`/
   `crypto.getRandomValues` outside `useMemo`.
5. **D3 AM — `useSequenceResources` + `DynamicSequenceRegistry`** —
   thin factories over the shared infrastructure, merged into
   `SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_REGISTRY` at runtime; force
   `browserRender:true, workerRender:false` for DB-stored rows.
6. **D3 PM — Unified UX.**
   - Add classifier prompt to existing generate flow (returns
     `{path: 'json'|'code', reason}`).
   - Add "fork to DB" action when path=code and source is
     theme-bundled.
   - Surface a path/capability badge on results.
   - Headless smoke render (1 frame) before allowing code-path Save.
7. **D4 — End-to-end.** Generate, edit, switch paths, insert,
   reload, confirm registry rehydrates. Document the contract.
   Parking lot: worker-side render, public sharing, promote-to-vendor.

---

## 4. What's still unanswered (carried from feasibility audit)

- Worker (Railway) support for compiling DB-stored sequence code.
  Out of scope for MVP; force `workerRender:false`.
- Whether `ParameterSchema` (effects) and JSON Schema (sequences)
  should ever merge. Don't decide in MVP.
- Promotion path from a DB-stored sequence back into
  `vendor/timeline-theme-2rp` / `banodoco-workspace`. Manual PR for
  now; document the rule.

# Implementation Plan: AI-Generated Sequence Components — Reuse Extraction + Codegen Pipeline

## Overview

Goal: ship D1 PM → D3 PM of the sequence-code-pipeline plan. We extract three shared modules from the effects AI pipeline, clone the edge function for sequence components, add validators, wire a `DynamicSequenceRegistry` into the existing `SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_REGISTRY`, and bolt a unified-UX classifier + fork-to-DB + path badge + smoke render onto the existing `SequenceCreatorPanel` flow.

Repo shape that matters:
- Effects compile + dynamic registry: `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileEffect.tsx`, `src/tools/video-editor/effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/effects/index.tsx`. Tests: `effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.test.tsx`.
- Effects edge function: `supabase/functions/ai-generate-effect/{index.ts, templates.ts}`. Shared utilities live in `supabase/functions/_shared/`.
- Sequences side: `src/tools/video-editor/sequences/registry.ts` (const `SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_REGISTRY`, capability builder), `sequences/components/ImageJumpSequence.tsx` (props shape `{clip, params, theme, fps}`), `sequences/metadata.ts` (`TRUSTED_SEQUENCE_METADATA`).
- Sequence panel + JSON-only AI flow: `components/SequenceCreator/SequenceCreatorPanel.tsx`, `components/SequenceCreator/sequenceGenerationService.ts`, edge function `supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence/`.
- Resources hook: `src/features/resources/hooks/useResources.ts` (`ResourceType` union at line 94, `EffectMetadata` at 80, `useListResources`/`useListPublicResources`). Effect catalog hook: `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useEffectResources.ts` — direct template for `useSequenceResources`.

Hard constraints from the brief:
- Three shared modules live at `src/tools/video-editor/runtime-components/{compileWithGlobals.ts,DynamicComponentRegistry.ts,index.ts}` and `supabase/functions/_shared/{aiCodegenRetry.ts,promptEnvelope.ts}`.
- DB-stored sequence rows must force `browserRender:true, workerRender:false`.
- Skip D1 AM (manual contract spike) and D4 (manual E2E).

Skipping D1 AM means the sequence-component runtime contract (props shape, globals tuple, JSX-OK assumption) is uncalibrated — see `assumptions` and `questions`. We code against the existing `ImageJumpSequence` shape + `@banodoco/timeline-composition/theme-api` surface as the de-facto contract; if the spike later finds a mismatch, only `compileSequenceComponent.tsx` and the prompt's globals/output rules need to move.

---

## Phase 1: D1 PM — Extract shared modules

### Step 1: Extract the compile harness (`src/tools/video-editor/runtime-components/compileWithGlobals.ts`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `src/tools/video-editor/runtime-components/compileWithGlobals.ts` (new file). Lift the Sucrase init + IIFE wrapper + `new Function` harness verbatim from `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileEffect.tsx:6-86`, but parameterized:
   - `compileWithGlobals<TProps>(code: string, globals: Record<string, unknown>, options?: { transforms?: Array<'jsx'|'typescript'>, jsxRuntime?: 'classic'|'automatic' }): CompileResult<FC<TProps>>`
   - Sync entry `compileWithGlobalsSync` (mirrors `compileEffect`); async entry `compileWithGlobalsAsync` (mirrors `compileEffectAsync`); preload `preloadSucrase()`.
   - Lazy-loaded `transformSync` cache lives once in this module (eliminates the duplicate Sucrase load the brief flags).
   - Apply globals by computing `Object.keys(globals)` and `Object.values(globals)` so the order matches between `new Function(...keys, body)` and `factory(...values)` — eliminates the manual tuple drift risk.
2. **Refactor** `src/tools/video-editor/effects/compileEffect.tsx` into a 30-line wrapper:
   - Imports `compileWithGlobalsAsync` / `compileWithGlobalsSync` / `preloadSucrase` from `runtime-components/compileWithGlobals`.
   - Defines the effect globals object `{ React, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, interpolate, spring, AbsoluteFill, useAudioReactive, useAudioParam }` once.
   - Re-exports `compileEffect`, `compileEffectAsync`, `tryCompileEffectAsync`, `preloadSucrase`, `CompileResult` so existing imports keep working (`DynamicEffectRegistry.ts:3`, `effects/index.tsx`, tests at `effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.test.tsx:19`).

### Step 2: Generalize the registry (`src/tools/video-editor/runtime-components/DynamicComponentRegistry.ts`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `runtime-components/DynamicComponentRegistry.ts` by hoisting `src/tools/video-editor/effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.ts` (all 121 lines) into a generic class:
   ```ts
   class DynamicComponentRegistry<TProps, TSchema = unknown> {
     constructor(opts: {
       builtIn: Record<string, FC<TProps>>;
       compile: (code: string) => FC<TProps>;
       compileAsync: (code: string) => Promise<FC<TProps>>;
       schemasEqual?: (a: TSchema | undefined, b: TSchema | undefined) => boolean;
       normalizeName?: (name: string) => string;  // default: strip 'custom:' prefix
     }) { ... }
     // subscribe / getSnapshot / batch / register / registerAsync / unregister /
     // get / getCode / getSchema / listAll / isDynamic / getAllDynamicCode
   }
   ```
   Copy the exact stale-pending-async guard from `DynamicEffectRegistry.ts:55-66` (load-bearing for the test at `DynamicEffectRegistry.test.tsx:251-291`).
2. **Refactor** `src/tools/video-editor/effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.ts` into a 10-line factory: `class DynamicEffectRegistry extends DynamicComponentRegistry<EffectComponentProps, ParameterSchema>` with the constructor wiring `compile: compileEffect, compileAsync: compileEffectAsync`. Keep the exported class name and signature — every callsite (`effects/index.tsx:3,80-93`, `hooks/useEffectRegistry.ts`, the test file) keeps working.
3. **Create** `runtime-components/index.ts` re-exporting `DynamicComponentRegistry`, `compileWithGlobals`, `compileWithGlobalsSync`, `compileWithGlobalsAsync`, `preloadSucrase`, `CompileResult`.

### Step 3: Confirm the effects pipeline still passes (`src/tools/video-editor/effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.test.tsx`)

**Scope:** Small

1. **Run** `npx vitest run src/tools/video-editor/effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.test.tsx` — all 11 cases must still pass without changes (the test imports are unchanged because the wrapper preserves the `DynamicEffectRegistry` and `compileEffect` exports).
2. **Add** `src/tools/video-editor/runtime-components/DynamicComponentRegistry.test.tsx` with two minimal cases: (a) register/get a generic `<TProps={a:number}>` component, (b) async stale-write guard fires for the new generic class too.

### Step 4: Extract edge-side shared helpers (`supabase/functions/_shared/`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `supabase/functions/_shared/promptEnvelope.ts`:
   - `parseEnvelope(text: string, fields: readonly string[]): { values: Record<string, string|null>; codeBody: string; ranges: TextRange[] }` — a generalization of `extractEffectCodeAndMeta` (`ai-generate-effect/templates.ts:417-443`). Split the per-line `// FIELD: value` parser (`templates.ts:235-283`) and the `findBalancedJsonArray` helper (lines 285-342) into reusable functions; the array reader is invoked when a field name is in a `jsonArrayFields` whitelist passed by the caller.
   - Export `stripMarkdownFences` (verbatim from `templates.ts:227-233`) and `stripRanges` (verbatim from lines 393-399).
   - Do NOT export the auto-typo-fix or `KNOWN_TYPOS` — those stay domain-specific in each function's `templates.ts` (per Bucket 3 of the brief).
2. **Create** `supabase/functions/_shared/aiCodegenRetry.ts`:
   - `attemptSelfInvokeRetry({ req, functionName, retryDepth, maxDepth, payload, parseError, rawOutput, logger }): Promise<Response | null>` — encapsulates `ai-generate-effect/index.ts:228-263`. Returns `null` if `retryDepth >= maxDepth` so the caller can fall through to the original 422 response. Forwards the `Authorization` header and falls back to `SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY`.
3. **Refactor** `supabase/functions/ai-generate-effect/index.ts` to use `attemptSelfInvokeRetry` (replaces lines 228-263) and `supabase/functions/ai-generate-effect/templates.ts:417-423` to use `parseEnvelope` (auto-typo-fix and `validateExtractedEffectCode` stay in `templates.ts`). Net: ~80 LOC drop in `ai-generate-effect/index.ts`.
4. **Sanity check** `deno check supabase/functions/ai-generate-effect/index.ts supabase/functions/_shared/aiCodegenRetry.ts supabase/functions/_shared/promptEnvelope.ts`.

---

## Phase 2: D2 — New edge function + validators

### Step 5: Clone the edge function (`supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component/`)

**Scope:** Large

1. **Create** `supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component/index.ts` modeled on `ai-generate-effect/index.ts` (post-refactor). Differences:
   - `ANTHROPIC_MODEL = "claude-opus-4-6"` (same), `RATE_LIMITS.expensive` (same), uses `attemptSelfInvokeRetry` + `parseEnvelope`.
   - Body: `{ prompt, name?, themeId?, existingComponent?: { code, schema, defaults }, _retryDepth?, _retryError?, _retryFailedCode? }` (replaces `existingCode`).
   - Imports `buildGenerateSequenceComponentMessages`, `extractSequenceComponentCodeAndMeta` from a sibling `templates.ts`.
   - Response: `{ code, name, description, schemaJson, defaultsJson, message, model }`.
2. **Create** `supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component/templates.ts`:
   - Define the `SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_CONTRACT` doc string for the prompt: props are `{ clip: ResolvedTimelineClip, params: object, theme: RuntimeTheme, fps: number }`, default export is a function component returning a Remotion tree, JSX is **allowed**, no imports, no exports (assign to `exports.default`).
   - `AVAILABLE_SEQUENCE_GLOBALS` listing the 14 globals: `React, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig, interpolate, spring, AbsoluteFill, Sequence, Series, Img, Video, Audio, Easing, useTheme, composeAnimations` (locks the contract — also pinned in Step 6 below; flag in `assumptions`).
   - `OUTPUT_RULES` lifted from `ai-generate-effect/templates.ts:63-111` and adjusted: JSX OK, two extra envelope fields `// SCHEMA: <JSON Schema Draft 2020-12 object>` and `// DEFAULTS: <JSON object matching schema>`. Forbid `Math.random()` outside `useMemo`, `Date.now()`, `performance.now()`, `crypto.getRandomValues()` anywhere.
   - `extractSequenceComponentCodeAndMeta(text)` calls `parseEnvelope(text, ['NAME','DESCRIPTION','SCHEMA','DEFAULTS','MESSAGE'])`, parses `SCHEMA`/`DEFAULTS` as JSON objects (not arrays — use a `findBalancedJsonObject` variant added to `_shared/promptEnvelope.ts`), then runs `validateSequenceComponentCode(code, schema)`.
3. **Create** `supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component/sequence-component-validation.ts`:
   - `validateSequenceComponentCode(code: string, schema: object): void` — extends the effect ruleset.

### Step 6: Validators (`supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component/sequence-component-validation.ts`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Implement** the static rules (regex):
   - Must contain `exports.default =`.
   - Must not contain `import` / `export` statements (reuse the regex from `ai-generate-effect/templates.ts:467-473`).
   - Forbid `Date.now\(`, `performance\.now\(`, `crypto\.getRandomValues` anywhere; forbid `Math.random\(` outside `React.useMemo(...)` (reuse the strip-pattern from `templates.ts:482-484`).
2. **Implement** the AST param-coverage walker:
   - Use `https://esm.sh/acorn@8?target=denonext` (or `acorn` mirror Deno already permits — see existing `_shared/` for precedent) to parse the code with `{ ecmaVersion: 'latest', sourceType: 'script' }` after first running it through Sucrase to strip JSX/TS (or use `acorn-jsx` + `@babel/parser`'s loose mode; whichever is already in the Deno function deps — pick the lighter option, document in code).
   - Walk nodes; collect every `MemberExpression` whose object resolves to `params` (i.e., the function parameter named `params`, or destructured `{params}`). For each `params.X`, assert `X in schema.properties` AND `X in defaults`.
   - On failure throw `Error('Param "${X}" used in code but missing from schema/defaults')`.
3. **Add** `supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component/sequence-component-validation.test.ts` (Deno test): valid case, missing-from-schema case, `Date.now` rejection, `Math.random` outside `useMemo` rejection, `Math.random` inside `useMemo` accepted.
4. **Run** `deno test supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component/`.

## Phase 3: D3 AM — Resources hook + dynamic registry wiring

### Step 7: Add `sequence-component` resource type (`src/features/resources/hooks/useResources.ts`)

**Scope:** Small

1. **Extend** the `ResourceType` union (line 94) to include `'sequence-component'`.
2. **Extend** `isResourceType` (line 113-115) to include the new value.
3. **Add** `SequenceComponentMetadata` interface mirroring `EffectMetadata` (line 80-92):
   ```ts
   export interface SequenceComponentMetadata {
     name: string;
     slug: string;
     code: string;
     schemaJson: object;
     defaultsJson: object;
     clipType: string;
     themeId: string;
     description: string;
     created_by: { is_you: boolean; username?: string };
     is_public: boolean;
   }
   ```
4. **Extend** the `ResourceMetadata` union (line 95) with `SequenceComponentMetadata`.

### Step 8: Sequence resource catalog hook (`src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSequenceResources.ts`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/sequence-component-catalog.ts`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `src/tools/video-editor/lib/sequence-component-catalog.ts` mirroring `lib/effect-catalog.ts` (whatever `useEffectResources` imports from it). Export `SequenceComponentResource`, `VideoEditorSequenceComponentCatalog`, `createVideoEditorSequenceComponentCatalog({...})`.
2. **Create** `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSequenceResources.ts` as a near-clone of `hooks/useEffectResources.ts:1-196`. Keep the same `Provider` + `useResolvedXCatalog` + `useXResources` + `useCreateXResource`/`useUpdateXResource`/`useDeleteXResource` shape. The mutation wrappers call the underlying `useCreateResource`/`useUpdateResource`/`useDeleteResource` with `type: 'sequence-component'`.

### Step 9: Dynamic sequence registry (`src/tools/video-editor/sequences/compileSequenceComponent.tsx`, `sequences/DynamicSequenceRegistry.ts`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `src/tools/video-editor/sequences/compileSequenceComponent.tsx`:
   - Imports the 14 globals listed in Step 5 from `remotion`, `@banodoco/timeline-composition/theme-api`, and `react`.
   - Wraps `compileWithGlobalsSync` / `compileWithGlobalsAsync` from `runtime-components/compileWithGlobals`. Uses `transforms: ['jsx','typescript']`, `jsxRuntime: 'classic'`.
   - Exports `compileSequenceComponent`, `compileSequenceComponentAsync`, `tryCompileSequenceComponentAsync`.
   - The component-shape generic is `FC<{ clip: ResolvedTimelineClip; params?: Record<string, unknown>; theme?: RuntimeTheme; fps: number }>`.
2. **Create** `src/tools/video-editor/sequences/DynamicSequenceRegistry.ts` as a 10-line factory: `class DynamicSequenceRegistry extends DynamicComponentRegistry<SequenceComponentProps, object>` wired to `compileSequenceComponent` / `compileSequenceComponentAsync`.

### Step 10: Merge dynamic registry into the runtime view (`src/tools/video-editor/sequences/registry.ts`)

**Scope:** Medium — touches a const that has type-level consumers

1. **Keep** `LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY` and the const `SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_REGISTRY` for theme + local built-ins (other code does `keyof typeof SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_REGISTRY`).
2. **Add** `getResolvedSequenceClipCapabilityRegistry(dynamicEntries: Record<string, { source: string; themeId?: string }>): Record<string, ClipCapabilityDescriptor>`:
   - Starts from `SEQUENCE_CLIP_CAPABILITY_REGISTRY` (existing line 171-173 export).
   - Layers in dynamic entries with hard-coded capabilities `{ preview: 'browser', browserRender: true, workerRender: false, externalRender: false }` (force the worker-off rule on DB-stored rows).
   - Source label `'db-sequence-component'` (extend the `ClipCapabilitySource` union at line 27-32).
3. **Hook** the dynamic registry in at the React tree level: in the same provider that mounts `useSequenceResources` (Step 8), build a `DynamicSequenceRegistry` once and call `registerAsync(metadata.clipType, metadata.code, metadata.schemaJson)` for each catalog entry inside a `registry.batch(async () => …)`. Expose via context (mirror `useEffectRegistry` at `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useEffectRegistry.ts`).
4. **Update** the consumers that look up sequence components (search for usages of `SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_REGISTRY` and `getClipCapabilityDescriptor` — confirm that `describeClipCapability` at `registry.ts:247-256` is the read path; route it through the resolved-registry getter when a dynamic registry context is available).
5. **Lookup name**: prefix DB-stored sequence clipTypes with `custom:` in the timeline JSON (mirrors the effects `custom:` prefix pattern at `effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.ts:117-119`) so they cannot collide with theme-bundled clipTypes.

---

## Phase 4: D3 PM — Unified UX (classifier + fork + badge + smoke render)

### Step 11: Classifier preamble in the existing JSON-only flow (`supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence/templates.ts`, `supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence/index.ts`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Extend** the system prompt in `supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence/templates.ts` so the model's first output line is `// PATH: json` or `// PATH: code` followed by a `// REASON: ...` line, then the existing JSON drafts (or, if `// PATH: code`, ONLY those two lines). The classifier sees: current schema, current params, user prompt — already part of the sequence prompt context.
2. **Update** `supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence/index.ts` to read the preamble before `extractSequenceDrafts`. If `path === 'code'`, return `{ classifier: { path: 'code', reason } }` without drafts. If `path === 'json'`, strip the preamble and parse drafts as today.
3. **Front-end branch** in `src/tools/video-editor/components/SequenceCreator/sequenceGenerationService.ts:52-150`: when the response includes `classifier.path === 'code'`, dispatch a follow-up call to `ai-generate-sequence-component` with the same `prompt` + (if the selected clip resolves to a DB-stored row) `existingComponent`.

### Step 12: Fork-to-DB action + path badge (`src/tools/video-editor/components/SequenceCreator/SequenceCreatorPanel.tsx`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Add** a `forkToDb` flow when the classifier returns `path: 'code'` and the selected clip's source is `installed-sequence` (i.e. theme-bundled — see `registry.ts:139`): present a deliberate confirmation ("Customize this sequence for yourself"). On confirm, call the new `ai-generate-sequence-component` edge function with `existingComponent` derived from the bundled component's source (read via a new `getBundledComponentSource(clipType)` helper that returns the vendored TSX text + schema.json; for MVP we ship this only for `LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY` entries where source is bundled in-tree, and skip for purely theme-package entries — see `questions`).
2. **Render** a path/capability badge below the result preview: "Edited params" (path=json), "Generated component code · browser-only render" (path=code · DB-stored). Badge component lives co-located with the panel; uses Tailwind `bg-background`/`text-foreground` per CLAUDE.md.
3. **Surface** the badge from day 1 — never hide it.

### Step 13: Headless smoke render gate (`src/tools/video-editor/sequences/headlessRender.ts`)

**Scope:** Medium

1. **Create** `src/tools/video-editor/sequences/headlessRender.ts` exporting `smokeRenderSequenceComponent({ code, schemaJson, defaultsJson, themeId, fps }): Promise<{ ok: true } | { ok: false; error: string }>`:
   - Calls `compileSequenceComponentAsync` to compile.
   - Renders one frame (`useCurrentFrame` mocked to `0`, `useVideoConfig` returning a 320×320×30fps stub) inside a detached `react-dom/server.renderToString` (no Remotion Player needed for a 1-frame static render) wrapped in an error boundary; if it throws, return `{ ok: false, error }`. 5-second timeout via `Promise.race`.
2. **Gate** the panel's Save action: before persisting via `useCreateSequenceResource` / `useUpdateSequenceResource`, run `smokeRenderSequenceComponent`; on failure surface the error inline (panel-local error state — no toast, per CLAUDE.md "errors only / no success toasts"; but errors here are panel-inline, not toast). Do NOT save if smoke render fails.
3. **Add** unit test `src/tools/video-editor/sequences/headlessRender.test.ts`: (a) a known-good sample component renders; (b) a deliberately broken component returns `{ ok: false }`.

---

## Execution Order

1. Phase 1 (D1 PM) lands first end-to-end — extracted modules + verified effect tests pass — because everything else imports it.
2. Phase 2 (D2) — edge function + validators — independent of front-end work and validatable with `deno test`.
3. Phase 3 (D3 AM) — front-end resources hook + dynamic registry wiring.
4. Phase 4 (D3 PM) — classifier + fork-to-DB + badge + smoke render gate. The smoke render gate (Step 13) lands before the Save button is enabled for code-path results.

## Validation Order

1. After Phase 1: `npx vitest run src/tools/video-editor/effects/DynamicEffectRegistry.test.tsx src/tools/video-editor/runtime-components/`. Effects tests must be green.
2. After Phase 2: `deno test supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component/` and `deno check` on every touched edge file.
3. After Phase 3: `npx tsc --noEmit -p tsconfig.app.json` (the `ResourceType` union and `keyof typeof SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_REGISTRY` consumers will surface drift).
4. After Phase 4: `npx vitest run src/tools/video-editor/sequences/`. Then full `npx vitest run` once more to catch regressions across the editor.
5. Lint: `npx eslint src/tools/video-editor/runtime-components src/tools/video-editor/sequences src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useSequenceResources.ts src/features/resources/hooks/useResources.ts supabase/functions/_shared supabase/functions/ai-generate-sequence-component`.

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the grouped-drag plan against `useclipdrag` and `usetimelinetrackmanagement.movecliptorow()`. the plan fixes the pointer-down selection regression, but it still does not say how `pinnedshotgroup.trackid` is updated when a grouped drag or move lands on a different row, so the overlay and menu state can still point at the old track after a successful cross-track move.",

      "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 5.1 claims `updatepinnedgroupwithchildren` is 'currently imported by useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:357'. verified at useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:71-130 that this function is actually **defined locally** in useswitchtofinalvideo.ts, not imported from pinned-group-projection.ts. the real imports from pinned-group-projection at :5-8 are `buildgroupfromchildren` and `replacegroupchildren`, both of which are already in step 5's delete list. the plan's characterization is factually wrong (delete list shouldn't include updatepinnedgroupwithchildren as a pinned-group-projection export), but the substantive intent still works: once `buildgroupfromchildren`/`replacegroupchildren` are deleted, the local helper won't compile and step 11.2's 'inline soft-tag construction' rewrite removes it. minor wording error, not a blocking issue.",

  "find-the-callers-of-the-changed-function-what-arguments-do-they-actually-pass-does-the-fix-handle-all-of-them": [
    {
      "id": "DEBT-038",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the current callers of `isrecord` from `llm/messages.ts`. `index.ts` uses it to validate `rawsession`, `db.ts` uses it on db rows, `tool-calls.ts` uses it on parsed tool-call payloads and nested `function` objects, and `selectedclips.ts` uses it while normalizing attachments, so preserving that import contract matters to four different call paths.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "cleanup-and-quality-pass-on-20260404-0138"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-095",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: the current `commitdata()` and history pipeline treat each `applyedit()` as its own commit boundary. because step 16 proposes a post-commit reconcile edit after deletions, the actual callers here matter: without `skiphistory` or transaction coalescing, one user action will produce two history events and likely two saves.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-096",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: `handleassetdrop()` and the external-drop wrapper are not pure builders; they immediately mutate editor state and call `applyedit()` per asset. that means the plan cannot literally reuse those callers for atomic shot-drop creation without first extracting a lower-level helper, or it will violate its own \"single applyedit\" requirement.",
      "occurrence_count": 2,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-111",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the actual commit/history callers around step 18. `commitdata()` emits `beforecommit` and schedules save on every edit, and `usetimelinehistory.onbeforecommit()` pushes undo entries per commit boundary, so a reconcile-after-delete `applyedit` still fragments one user action into multiple history/save operations unless the plan also changes those caller contracts.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-115",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the multi-drag callers in `useclipdrag`. the 'new track' grouped-drag branch still routes through `buildconfigfromdragresult()` and `applyedit({ type: 'config' })`, so the revised plan still does not fully handle every caller shape for grouped moves once pinned-group metadata becomes part of the timeline state.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-two-tier-shot-20260406-1054"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-130",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the `usepinnedgroupsync()` call site in `src/tools/video-editor/components/timelineeditor/timelineeditor.tsx:479` against the proposed context-based interaction guard. because sync is called directly from `timelineeditor`, routing its guard through new editor-context plumbing is not the most direct fit for the actual caller and depends on extra provider/type changes that the step does not enumerate.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "refactor-pinned-shot-groups-20260408-0508"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-138",
      "concern": "find the callers of the changed function. what arguments do they actually pass? does the fix handle all of them?: checked the caller contract of `useselectedmediaclips()` via [usetimelineeditordata](/users/user_c042661f/documents/reigh-workspace/reigh-app/src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useselectedmediaclips.ts#l29). this hook's caller currently provides `selectedclipids` and `resolvedconfig`, not a resolved config object with `pinnedshotgroups`, so the plan still does not handle the actual arguments passed at that call site and needs to pull pinned groups from editor `data` instead.",
      "occurrence_count": 1,
      "plan_ids": [
        "implement-first-class-shot-20260409-2052"
      ]
    },
    {
      "id": "DEBT-158",

      {
        "id": "DEBT-041",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the actual export surface in `supabase/functions/ai-timeline-agent/llm/messages.ts` and its consumers. step 6 says to 'remove local `isrecord`' from `messages.ts` and import it from `utils.ts`, but without also preserving `messages.ts` as an exporter or rewiring `index.ts`, `db.ts`, `tool-calls.ts`, and `selectedclips.ts`, that change would break the module graph immediately.",

      {
        "id": "DEBT-055",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the render-gating cleanup plan against remotion's runtime exports in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/cancel-render.js:46-70`. `cancelrender()` stores an error and throws to abort the render; it is not a benign cleanup for an abandoned `delayrender` handle, so step 2's instruction to call `cancelrender(handle)` on unmount is technically wrong and would abort preview or export with an error.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-056",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked how remotion itself acquires delay handles in `node_modules/remotion/dist/cjs/iframe.js:8-18`. remotion's own components create the handle synchronously with `usestate(() => delayrender(...))`; the plan says 'calls delayrender() on mount', which reads like an effect-time action and leaves the initial-frame blocking behavior underspecified compared with the actual library pattern.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-audio-reactive-effects-to-20260406-0440"
        ]
      },

      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-070",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked revised step 2.4 against `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useclipresize.ts:76-100`. the plan still says to clamp audio by writing back to `action.end`, but the current hook treats the callback `action` as input data and then creates fresh row actions from it. mutating that caller-owned object is unnecessary and risks coupling the resize hook to outside references instead of using a local `clampedend` variable.",

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the no-duration-fetch decision against `src/tools/travel-between-images/hooks/video/useshotfinalvideos.ts:19-37`, `src/tools/video-editor/lib/config-utils.ts:17-35`, and `src/tools/video-editor/compositions/visualclip.tsx:114-123`. the repository still derives clip length from `to - from`, and the final-video query still provides no duration metadata, so the revised plan still cannot prove that `to: endtime - starttime` is safe for shorter source media; a short final video can still be asked to play longer than its actual content.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-a-final-video-available-20260406-0801"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-077",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: checked the hand-built replacement clip against the existing insert path in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/useassetmanagement.ts:201-252,262-271` and serialization in `src/tools/video-editor/hooks/usetimelinecommit.ts:210-216`. the revised `timelineeditor` sketch still bypasses current insertion invariants such as manual-track geometry defaults and explicit `cliporder` updates, so replacement clips on manual visual tracks will not be initialized the same way as other inserted video clips and may serialize in a different per-track order.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,
        "plan_ids": [
          "add-a-final-video-available-20260406-0801"
        ]
      },
      {
        "id": "DEBT-089",
        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: the new `pinnedshotgroupsoverride` design is still underspecified against `materializedata()`. `assembletimelinedata()` computes `stablesignature` from the config it receives, so if the executor literally follows step 2.4 and overwrites `pinnedshotgroups` on an already-materialized `timelinedata` before `commitdata()`, the saved config and the cached `stablesignature` will diverge unless the data is rebuilt from the updated config.",
        "occurrence_count": 1,

        "concern": "are the proposed changes technically correct?: step 5.1 claims `updatepinnedgroupwithchildren` is 'currently imported by useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:357'. verified at useswitchtofinalvideo.ts:71-130 that this function is actually **defined locally** in useswitchtofinalvideo.ts, not imported from pinned-group-projection.ts. the real imports from pinned-group-projection at :5-8 are `buildgroupfromchildren` and `replacegroupchildren`, both of which are already in step 5's delete list. the plan's characterization is factually wrong (delete list shouldn't include updatepinnedgroupwithchildren as a pinned-group-projection export), but the substantive intent still works: once `buildgroupfromchildren`/`replacegroupchildren` are deleted, the local helper won't compile and step 11.2's 'inline soft-tag construction' rewrite removes it. minor wording error, not a blocking issue.",

1	import React, { type FC } from 'react';
2	import { AbsoluteFill, interpolate, spring, useCurrentFrame, useVideoConfig } from 'remotion';
3	import type { EffectComponentProps } from '@/tools/video-editor/effects/entrances';
4	import { useAudioParam, useAudioReactive } from '@/tools/video-editor/effects/useAudioReactive';
5	
6	let transformSync: typeof import('sucrase').transform | null = null;
7	
8	export type CompileResult =
9	  | { ok: true; component: FC<EffectComponentProps> }
10	  | { ok: false; error: string };
11	
12	async function getTransform() {
13	  if (!transformSync) {
14	    const sucrase = await import('sucrase');
15	    transformSync = sucrase.transform;
16	  }
17	
18	  return transformSync;
19	}
20	
21	const CompileErrorEffect: FC<EffectComponentProps & { error: string }> = ({ children }) => {
22	  return <>{children}</>;
23	};
24	
25	function createFailedEffect(message: string): FC<EffectComponentProps> {
26	  return function FailedEffect(props: EffectComponentProps) {
27	    return <CompileErrorEffect {...props} error={`Custom effect compilation failed:\n${message}`} />;
28	  };
29	}
30	
31	function tryCompileWithTransform(
32	  code: string,
33	  transform: typeof import('sucrase').transform,
34	): CompileResult {
35	  try {
36	    const result = transform(code, {
37	      transforms: ['jsx', 'typescript'],
38	      jsxRuntime: 'classic',
39	      production: true,
40	    });
41	
42	    const wrappedCode = `
43	      var exports = {};
44	      var module = { exports: exports };
45	      ${result.code}
46	      return exports.default || module.exports.default || module.exports;
47	    `;
48	
49	    const factory = new Function(
50	      'React',
51	      'useCurrentFrame',
52	      'useVideoConfig',
53	      'interpolate',
54	      'spring',
55	      'AbsoluteFill',
56	      'useAudioReactive',
57	      'useAudioParam',
58	      wrappedCode,
59	    ) as (...args: unknown[]) => unknown;
60	
61	    const component = factory(
62	      React,
63	      useCurrentFrame,
64	      useVideoConfig,
65	      interpolate,
66	      spring,
67	      AbsoluteFill,
68	      useAudioReactive,
69	      useAudioParam,
70	    );
71	
72	    if (typeof component !== 'function') {
73	      throw new Error('Effect code did not produce a valid component (expected a function as default export)');
74	    }
75	
76	    return {
77	      ok: true,
78	      component: component as FC<EffectComponentProps>,
79	    };
80	  } catch (error) {
81	    return {
82	      ok: false,
83	      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),
84	    };
85	  }
86	}
87	
88	function compileWithTransform(
89	  code: string,
90	  transform: typeof import('sucrase').transform,
91	): FC<EffectComponentProps> {
92	  const result = tryCompileWithTransform(code, transform);
93	  return result.ok ? result.component : createFailedEffect(result.error);
94	}
95	
96	export async function preloadSucrase(): Promise<void> {
97	  await getTransform();
98	}
99	
100	export function compileEffect(code: string): FC<EffectComponentProps> {
101	  if (!transformSync) {
102	    return createFailedEffect('Sucrase is not loaded yet.');
103	  }
104	
105	  return compileWithTransform(code, transformSync);
106	}
107	
108	export async function tryCompileEffectAsync(code: string): Promise<CompileResult> {
109	  return tryCompileWithTransform(code, await getTransform());
110	}
111	
112	export async function compileEffectAsync(code: string): Promise<FC<EffectComponentProps>> {
113	  const result = await tryCompileEffectAsync(code);
114	  return result.ok ? result.component : createFailedEffect(result.error);
115	}

1	import type { FC } from 'react';
2	import type { EffectComponentProps } from '@/tools/video-editor/effects/entrances';
3	import { compileEffect, compileEffectAsync } from '@/tools/video-editor/effects/compileEffect';
4	import type { ParameterSchema } from '@/tools/video-editor/types';
5	
6	type DynamicEffectRecord = {
7	  component: FC<EffectComponentProps>;
8	  code: string;
9	  schema?: ParameterSchema;
10	};
11	
12	export class DynamicEffectRegistry {
13	  private builtIn: Record<string, FC<EffectComponentProps>>;
14	  private dynamic: Record<string, DynamicEffectRecord> = {};
15	  private pendingAsync: Record<string, { code: string; schema?: ParameterSchema }> = {};
16	  private _version = 0;
17	  private _batchDepth = 0;
18	  private _pendingNotify = false;
19	  private _listeners = new Set<() => void>();
20	
21	  constructor(builtIn: Record<string, FC<EffectComponentProps>>) {
22	    this.builtIn = { ...builtIn };
23	  }
24	
25	  subscribe = (listener: () => void): (() => void) => {
26	    this._listeners.add(listener);
27	    return () => this._listeners.delete(listener);
28	  };
29	
30	  getSnapshot = (): number => this._version;
31	
32	  async batch(fn: () => void | Promise<void>): Promise<void> {
33	    this._batchDepth += 1;
34	    try {
35	      await fn();
36	    } finally {
37	      this._batchDepth -= 1;
38	      if (this._batchDepth === 0 && this._pendingNotify) {
39	        this._pendingNotify = false;
40	        this._notify();
41	      }
42	    }
43	  }
44	
45	  register(name: string, code: string, schema?: ParameterSchema): void {
46	    const normalized = this.normalizeName(name);
47	    const existing = this.dynamic[normalized];
48	    if (existing?.code === code && this.schemasEqual(existing.schema, schema)) return;
49	    delete this.pendingAsync[normalized];
50	    const component = compileEffect(code);
51	    this.dynamic[normalized] = { component, code, schema };
52	    this._notify();
53	  }
54	
55	  async registerAsync(name: string, code: string, schema?: ParameterSchema): Promise<void> {
56	    const normalized = this.normalizeName(name);
57	    const existing = this.dynamic[normalized];
58	    if (existing?.code === code && this.schemasEqual(existing.schema, schema)) return;
59	    this.pendingAsync[normalized] = { code, schema };
60	    const component = await compileEffectAsync(code);
61	    const pending = this.pendingAsync[normalized];
62	    if (!pending || pending.code !== code || !this.schemasEqual(pending.schema, schema)) return;
63	    delete this.pendingAsync[normalized];
64	    this.dynamic[normalized] = { component, code, schema };
65	    this._notify();
66	  }
67	
68	  unregister(name: string): void {
69	    const normalized = this.normalizeName(name);
70	    if (!(normalized in this.dynamic)) return;
71	    delete this.pendingAsync[normalized];
72	    delete this.dynamic[normalized];
73	    this._notify();
74	  }
75	
76	  get(name: string): FC<EffectComponentProps> | undefined {
77	    const normalized = this.normalizeName(name);
78	    return this.builtIn[normalized] ?? this.dynamic[normalized]?.component;
79	  }
80	
81	  getCode(name: string): string | undefined {
82	    return this.dynamic[this.normalizeName(name)]?.code;
83	  }
84	
85	  getSchema(name: string): ParameterSchema | undefined {
86	    return this.dynamic[this.normalizeName(name)]?.schema;
87	  }
88	
89	  listAll(): string[] {
90	    return [...new Set([...Object.keys(this.builtIn), ...Object.keys(this.dynamic)])];
91	  }
92	
93	  isDynamic(name: string): boolean {
94	    const normalized = this.normalizeName(name);
95	    return normalized in this.dynamic && !(normalized in this.builtIn);
96	  }
97	
98	  getAllDynamicCode(): Record<string, string> {
99	    return Object.fromEntries(
100	      Object.entries(this.dynamic).map(([name, { code }]) => [name, code]),
101	    );
102	  }
103	
104	  private schemasEqual(a?: ParameterSchema, b?: ParameterSchema): boolean {
105	    return a === b || JSON.stringify(a) === JSON.stringify(b);
106	  }
107	
108	  private _notify(): void {
109	    if (this._batchDepth > 0) {
110	      this._pendingNotify = true;
111	      return;
112	    }
113	    this._version += 1;
114	    this._listeners.forEach((listener) => listener());
115	  }
116	
117	  private normalizeName(name: string): string {
118	    return name.startsWith('custom:') ? name.slice(7) : name;
119	  }
120	}
121

2	import { serve } from "https://deno.land/std@0.224.0/http/server.ts";
3	import {
4	  enforceRateLimit,
5	  RATE_LIMITS,
6	} from "../_shared/rateLimit.ts";
7	import { bootstrapEdgeHandler, NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS } from "../_shared/edgeHandler.ts";
8	import { jsonResponse } from "../_shared/http.ts";
9	import { toErrorMessage } from "../_shared/errorMessage.ts";
10	import {
11	  buildGenerateEffectMessages,
12	  extractEffectCodeAndMeta,
13	  extractQuestionResponse,
14	  type EffectCategory,
15	} from "./templates.ts";
16	
17	// ── Models ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
18	// All generation (create + edit + retry) uses Claude Opus 4.6 via Anthropic
19	const ANTHROPIC_MODEL = "claude-opus-4-6";
20	const ANTHROPIC_URL = "https://api.anthropic.com/v1/messages";
21	const ANTHROPIC_TIMEOUT_MS = 150_000; // max out to edge function wall-clock limit
22	
23	const MAX_RETRY_DEPTH = 1; // max number of self-invocation retries
24	
25	const EFFECT_CATEGORIES: EffectCategory[] = ["entrance", "exit", "continuous"];
26	
27	function isEffectCategory(value: unknown): value is EffectCategory {
28	  return typeof value === "string" && EFFECT_CATEGORIES.includes(value as EffectCategory);
29	}
30	
31	// ── Response types ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
32	
33	interface LLMResponse {
34	  content: string;
35	  model: string;
36	}
37	
38	// ── Anthropic generation (Claude Opus 4.6) ──────────────────────────
39	
40	async function callAnthropic(
41	  messages: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>,
42	  logger: { info: (msg: string) => void },
43	): Promise<LLMResponse> {
44	  const apiKey = Deno.env.get("ANTHROPIC_API_KEY");
45	  if (!apiKey) throw new Error("[ai-generate-effect] Missing ANTHROPIC_API_KEY");
46	
47	  const controller = new AbortController();
48	  const timeout = setTimeout(() => controller.abort(), ANTHROPIC_TIMEOUT_MS);
49	
50	  // Separate system message from user/assistant messages (Anthropic API uses top-level system param)
51	  const systemContent = messages.find(m => m.role === "system")?.content;
52	  const chatMessages = messages.filter(m => m.role !== "system");
53	
54	  try {
55	    const startedAt = Date.now();
56	    logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] Anthropic streaming request: model=${ANTHROPIC_MODEL}`);
57	    const response = await fetch(ANTHROPIC_URL, {
58	      method: "POST",
59	      headers: {
60	        "x-api-key": apiKey,
61	        "anthropic-version": "2023-06-01",
62	        "Content-Type": "application/json",
63	      },
64	      body: JSON.stringify({
65	        model: ANTHROPIC_MODEL,
66	        max_tokens: 16384,
67	        temperature: 0.4,
68	        ...(systemContent ? { system: systemContent } : {}),
69	        messages: chatMessages,
70	        stream: true,
71	      }),
72	      signal: controller.signal,
73	    });
74	
75	    if (!response.ok) {
76	      const text = await response.text().catch(() => "");
77	      throw new Error(`Anthropic ${response.status}: ${text.slice(0, 500)}`);
78	    }
79	
80	    // Collect streamed SSE chunks into full content
81	    const reader = response.body!.getReader();
82	    const decoder = new TextDecoder();
83	    let content = "";
84	    let buffer = "";
85	
86	    while (true) {
87	      const { done, value } = await reader.read();
88	      if (done) break;
89	      buffer += decoder.decode(value, { stream: true });
90	
91	      const lines = buffer.split("\n");
92	      buffer = lines.pop() ?? "";
93	
94	      for (const line of lines) {
95	        if (!line.startsWith("data: ")) continue;
96	        const data = line.slice(6).trim();
97	        try {
98	          const chunk = JSON.parse(data);
99	          if (chunk.type === "content_block_delta" && chunk.delta?.type === "text_delta") {
100	            content += chunk.delta.text;
101	          }
102	        } catch {
103	          // skip malformed chunks
104	        }
105	      }
106	    }
107	
108	    content = content.trim();
109	    logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] Anthropic response in ${Date.now() - startedAt}ms, model=${ANTHROPIC_MODEL}, length=${content.length}`);
110	    return { content, model: ANTHROPIC_MODEL };
111	  } finally {
112	    clearTimeout(timeout);
113	  }
114	}
115	
116	// ── Main handler ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
117	
118	serve(async (req) => {
119	  const bootstrap = await bootstrapEdgeHandler(req, {
120	    functionName: "ai-generate-effect",
121	    logPrefix: "[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT]",
122	    parseBody: "strict",
123	    auth: {
124	      required: true,
125	      options: { allowJwtUserAuth: true },
126	    },
127	    ...NO_SESSION_RUNTIME_OPTIONS,
128	  });
129	  if (!bootstrap.ok) {
130	    return bootstrap.response;
131	  }
132	
133	  const { supabaseAdmin, logger, auth, body } = bootstrap.value;
134	  if (!auth?.userId) {
135	    return jsonResponse({ error: "Authentication failed" }, 401);
136	  }
137	
138	  const rateLimitDenied = await enforceRateLimit({
139	    supabaseAdmin,
140	    functionName: "ai-generate-effect",
141	    userId: auth.userId,
142	    config: RATE_LIMITS.expensive,
143	    logger,
144	    logPrefix: "[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT]",
145	    responses: {
146	      serviceUnavailable: () => jsonResponse({ error: "Rate limit service unavailable" }, 503),
147	    },
148	  });
149	  if (rateLimitDenied) {
150	    return rateLimitDenied;
151	  }
152	
153	  const prompt = typeof body.prompt === "string" ? body.prompt.trim() : "";
154	  const effectName = typeof body.name === "string" ? body.name.trim() : "";
155	  const category = body.category;
156	  const existingCode = typeof body.existingCode === "string" && body.existingCode.trim()
157	    ? body.existingCode
158	    : undefined;
159	
160	  if (!prompt) {
161	    return jsonResponse({ error: "prompt is required" }, 400);
162	  }
163	
164	  if (!isEffectCategory(category)) {
165	    return jsonResponse({ error: `category must be one of: ${EFFECT_CATEGORIES.join(", ")}` }, 400);
166	  }
167	
168	  const isEditMode = Boolean(existingCode);
169	  const retryDepth = typeof body._retryDepth === "number" ? body._retryDepth : 0;
170	  const retryError = typeof body._retryError === "string" ? body._retryError : undefined;
171	  const retryFailedCode = typeof body._retryFailedCode === "string" ? body._retryFailedCode : undefined;
172	
173	  try {
174	    // If this is a retry invocation, build edit-mode messages to fix the failed code
175	    let messages: Array<{ role: string; content: string }>;
176	
177	    if (retryDepth > 0 && retryFailedCode && retryError) {
178	      logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] retry depth=${retryDepth} — fixing: ${retryError}`);
179	      const retryInput = buildGenerateEffectMessages({
180	        prompt,
181	        name: effectName || undefined,
182	        category,
183	        existingCode: retryFailedCode,
184	        validationError: retryError,
185	      });
186	      messages = [
187	        { role: "system", content: retryInput.systemMsg },
188	        { role: "user", content: retryInput.userMsg },
189	      ];
190	    } else {
191	      const { systemMsg, userMsg } = buildGenerateEffectMessages({
192	        prompt,
193	        name: effectName || undefined,
194	        category,
195	        existingCode,
196	      });
197	      messages = [
198	        { role: "system", content: systemMsg },
199	        { role: "user", content: userMsg },
200	      ];
201	    }
202	
203	    // All generation uses Claude Opus 4.6 via Anthropic
204	    logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] ${retryDepth > 0 ? `retry(${retryDepth})` : isEditMode ? "edit" : "create"} → ${ANTHROPIC_MODEL} (Anthropic)`);
205	    await logger.flush();
206	    const llmResponse = await callAnthropic(messages, logger);
207	
208	    logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] raw output length=${llmResponse.content.length}, first 200 chars: ${llmResponse.content.slice(0, 200)}`);
209	
210	    const questionResponse = extractQuestionResponse(llmResponse.content);
211	    if (questionResponse) {
212	      logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] question response detected, returning conversational reply (length=${questionResponse.message.length})`);
213	      await logger.flush();
214	      return jsonResponse({
215	        message: questionResponse.message,
216	        isQuestionResponse: true,
217	        model: llmResponse.model,
218	      });
219	    }
220	
221	    let extracted;
222	    try {
223	      extracted = extractEffectCodeAndMeta(llmResponse.content);
224	    } catch (parseErr: unknown) {
225	      const parseMsg = parseErr instanceof Error ? parseErr.message : String(parseErr);
226	      logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] extraction/validation failed: ${parseMsg}`);
227	
228	      // Self-invoke a new instance to retry with the failed code as edit context
229	      if (retryDepth < MAX_RETRY_DEPTH) {
230	        logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] spawning retry invocation (depth ${retryDepth + 1})`);
231	        await logger.flush();
232	
233	        const supabaseUrl = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_URL");
234	        const serviceKey = Deno.env.get("SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY");
235	        if (!supabaseUrl || !serviceKey) {
236	          return jsonResponse({ error: parseMsg, rawOutput: llmResponse.content.slice(0, 500) }, 422);
237	        }
238	
239	        // Forward the original auth header so the retry is authenticated
240	        const authHeader = req.headers.get("Authorization") ?? `Bearer ${serviceKey}`;
241	        const retryResponse = await fetch(`${supabaseUrl}/functions/v1/ai-generate-effect`, {
242	          method: "POST",
243	          headers: {
244	            "Authorization": authHeader,
245	            "Content-Type": "application/json",
246	          },
247	          body: JSON.stringify({
248	            prompt,
249	            name: effectName || undefined,
250	            category,
251	            _retryDepth: retryDepth + 1,
252	            _retryError: parseMsg,
253	            _retryFailedCode: llmResponse.content,
254	          }),
255	        });
256	
257	        // Pass through the retry response directly
258	        const retryBody = await retryResponse.text();
259	        return new Response(retryBody, {
260	          status: retryResponse.status,
261	          headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json", "Access-Control-Allow-Origin": "*" },
262	        });
263	      }
264	
265	      logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] max retry depth reached, returning error`);
266	      logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] final output: ${llmResponse.content.slice(0, 1000)}`);
267	      await logger.flush();
268	      return jsonResponse({ error: parseMsg, rawOutput: llmResponse.content.slice(0, 500) }, 422);
269	    }
270	
271	    const { code, name: generatedName, description, parameterSchema, message } = extracted;
272	
273	    if (retryDepth > 0) {
274	      logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] retry succeeded at depth ${retryDepth}`);
275	    }
276	    await logger.flush();
277	    return jsonResponse({
278	      code,
279	      name: generatedName,
280	      description,
281	      parameterSchema,
282	      message: message || undefined,
283	      model: llmResponse.model,
284	    });
285	  } catch (err: unknown) {
286	    const message = toErrorMessage(err);
287	    console.error("[ai-generate-effect] Error generating effect:", message);
288	    logger.info(`[AI-GENERATE-EFFECT] error: ${message}`);
289	    await logger.flush();
290	    return jsonResponse({ error: "Internal server error", details: message }, 500);
291	  }
292	});
293

218	  | { status: 'unavailable'; metadata: TrustedSequenceMetadata }
219	  | { status: 'unknown'; clipType: string | undefined } => {

230	  if (trusted) {

232	  }
233

61	function useSupabaseEffectCatalog(
62	  userId: string | null | undefined,
63	  options?: { enabled?: boolean },
64	): VideoEditorEffectCatalog {
65	  const enabled = options?.enabled ?? true;
66	  const privateEffectsQuery = useListResources('effect', { enabled: enabled && Boolean(userId) });
67	  const publicEffectsQuery = useListPublicResources('effect', { enabled });
68	  const createEffect = useCreateEffectResource();
69	  const updateEffect = useUpdateEffectResource();
70	  const deleteEffect = useDeleteEffectResource();
71	
72	  const effects = useMemo(() => {
73	    const privateResources = userId ? privateEffectsQuery.data ?? [] : [];
74	    const publicResources = publicEffectsQuery.data ?? [];
75	    const deduped = new Map<string, EffectResource>();
76	
77	    for (const resource of [...privateResources, ...publicResources]) {
78	      deduped.set(resource.id, toEffectResource(resource));
79	    }
80	
81	    return [...deduped.values()];
82	  }, [privateEffectsQuery.data, publicEffectsQuery.data, userId]);
83	
84	  return useMemo(() => createVideoEditorEffectCatalog({
85	    effects,
86	    isLoading: privateEffectsQuery.isLoading || publicEffectsQuery.isLoading,
87	    isFetching: privateEffectsQuery.isFetching || publicEffectsQuery.isFetching,
88	    error: privateEffectsQuery.error ?? publicEffectsQuery.error ?? null,
89	    refetch: async () => Promise.all([privateEffectsQuery.refetch(), publicEffectsQuery.refetch()]),
90	    createEffect: async (variables) => {
91	      const resource = await createEffect.mutateAsync(variables);
92	      return { id: resource.id };
93	    },
94	    updateEffect: async (variables) => {
95	      const resource = await updateEffect.mutateAsync(variables);
96	      return { id: resource.id };
97	    },

99	  }), [

101	    deleteEffect,

103	    privateEffectsQuery.error,
104	    privateEffectsQuery.isFetching,
105	    privateEffectsQuery.isLoading,
106	    privateEffectsQuery.refetch,
107	    publicEffectsQuery.error,
108	    publicEffectsQuery.isFetching,
109	    publicEffectsQuery.isLoading,
110	    publicEffectsQuery.refetch,

1	-- Fix the RLS policy for public resources to work with both LoRAs and phase-configs
2	-- and to properly check boolean is_public field instead of string comparison
3	
4	-- Drop the old policy that had two bugs:
5	-- 1. Only allowed type = 'lora', not 'phase-config'
6	-- 2. Checked for string 'true' instead of boolean true
7	DROP POLICY IF EXISTS "Allow read access to public resources" ON public.resources;
8	
9	-- Create new policy that fixes both issues
10	CREATE POLICY "Allow read access to public resources" ON public.resources
11	FOR SELECT
12	USING (
13	  -- Allow any type (lora, phase-config, etc.) if is_public is true
14	  (metadata->>'is_public')::boolean = true
15	);
16	
17

221	const NAME_PATTERN = /^\s*\/\/\s*NAME\s*:\s*(.*)$/im;
222	const DESCRIPTION_PATTERN = /^\s*\/\/\s*DESCRIPTION\s*:\s*(.*)$/im;

227	function stripMarkdownFences(text: string): string {
228	  return text
229	    .trim()
230	    .replace(/^\s*```(?:tsx?|jsx?|javascript|typescript)?\s*$/gim, '')
231	    .replace(/^\s*```\s*$/gim, '')

234	
235	function getLineEnd(text: string, start: number): number {
236	  const newlineIndex = text.indexOf('\n', start);
237	  return newlineIndex === -1 ? text.length : newlineIndex + 1;
238	}
239	
240	function extractName(text: string): { name: string; range: TextRange | null } {
241	  const match = NAME_PATTERN.exec(text);
242	  if (!match || match.index === undefined) {
243	    return { name: '', range: null };
244	  }
245	
246	  return {
247	    name: match[1]?.trim() ?? '',
248	    range: {
249	      start: match.index,
250	      end: getLineEnd(text, match.index),
251	    },
252	  };
253	}
254	
255	function extractDescription(text: string): { description: string; range: TextRange | null } {
256	  const match = DESCRIPTION_PATTERN.exec(text);
257	  if (!match || match.index === undefined) {
258	    return { description: '', range: null };
259	  }
260	
261	  return {
262	    description: match[1]?.trim() ?? '',
263	    range: {
264	      start: match.index,
265	      end: getLineEnd(text, match.index),
266	    },
267	  };
268	}
269	
270	function extractMessage(text: string): { message: string; range: TextRange | null } {
271	  const match = MESSAGE_PATTERN.exec(text);
272	  if (!match || match.index === undefined) {
273	    return { message: '', range: null };
274	  }
275	
276	  return {
277	    message: match[1]?.trim() ?? '',
278	    range: {
279	      start: match.index,
280	      end: getLineEnd(text, match.index),
281	    },
282	  };
283	}
284	
285	function findBalancedJsonArray(text: string, startIndex: number): { raw: string; end: number } | null {
286	  let index = startIndex;
287	  while (index < text.length && /\s/.test(text[index] ?? '')) {
288	    index += 1;
289	  }
290	
291	  if (text[index] !== '[') {
292	    return null;
293	  }
294	
295	  let depth = 0;
296	  let inString = false;
297	  let isEscaped = false;
298	
299	  for (let cursor = index; cursor < text.length; cursor += 1) {
300	    const char = text[cursor];
301	
302	    if (inString) {
303	      if (isEscaped) {
304	        isEscaped = false;
305	        continue;
306	      }
307	
308	      if (char === '\\') {
309	        isEscaped = true;
310	        continue;
311	      }
312	
313	      if (char === '"') {
314	        inString = false;
315	      }
316	
317	      continue;
318	    }
319	
320	    if (char === '"') {
321	      inString = true;
322	      continue;
323	    }
324	
325	    if (char === '[') {
326	      depth += 1;
327	      continue;
328	    }
329	
330	    if (char === ']') {
331	      depth -= 1;
332	      if (depth === 0) {
333	        return {
334	          raw: text.slice(index, cursor + 1),
335	          end: cursor + 1,
336	        };
337	      }
338	    }
339	  }
340	
341	  return null;
342	}
343	
344	function sanitizeParameterSchema(value: unknown): ParameterDefinition[] {
345	  if (!Array.isArray(value)) {
346	    return [];
347	  }
348	
349	  return value.filter((entry): entry is ParameterDefinition => {
350	    return typeof entry === 'object' && entry !== null && typeof (entry as { name?: unknown }).name === 'string';
351	  });
352	}
353	
354	function extractParameterSchema(text: string): { parameterSchema: ParameterDefinition[]; range: TextRange | null } {
355	  const match = PARAMS_PATTERN.exec(text);
356	  if (!match || match.index === undefined) {
357	    return { parameterSchema: [], range: null };
358	  }
359	
360	  const markerStart = match.index;
361	  const markerEnd = match.index + match[0].length;
362	  const jsonArray = findBalancedJsonArray(text, markerEnd);
363	
364	  if (!jsonArray) {
365	    return {
366	      parameterSchema: [],
367	      range: {
368	        start: markerStart,
369	        end: getLineEnd(text, markerStart),
370	      },
371	    };
372	  }
373	
374	  try {
375	    return {
376	      parameterSchema: sanitizeParameterSchema(JSON.parse(jsonArray.raw)),
377	      range: {
378	        start: markerStart,
379	        end: jsonArray.end,
380	      },
381	    };
382	  } catch {
383	    return {
384	      parameterSchema: [],
385	      range: {
386	        start: markerStart,
387	        end: jsonArray.end,
388	      },
389	    };
390	  }
391	}

393	function stripRanges(text: string, ranges: Array<TextRange | null>): string {
394	  return ranges
395	    .filter((range): range is TextRange => range !== null)
396	    .sort((left, right) => right.start - left.start)
397	    .reduce((result, range) => result.slice(0, range.start) + result.slice(range.end), text)

407	  }

409	  const markerEnd = getLineEnd(normalized, match.index);
410	
411	  return {
412	    isQuestion: true,
413	    message: normalized.slice(markerEnd).trim(),
414	  };
415	}

417	export function extractEffectCodeAndMeta(responseText: string): ExtractedEffectMeta {
418	  const normalized = stripMarkdownFences(responseText);
419	  const { name, range: nameRange } = extractName(normalized);
420	  const { description, range: descriptionRange } = extractDescription(normalized);
421	  const { parameterSchema, range: parameterSchemaRange } = extractParameterSchema(normalized);
422	  const { message, range: messageRange } = extractMessage(normalized);
423	  const rawCode = stripRanges(normalized, [nameRange, descriptionRange, parameterSchemaRange, messageRange]);
424	
425	  // Auto-fix common LLM typos before validation
426	  const code = rawCode
427	    .replace(/\buseCurrentFrames\b/g, 'useCurrentFrame')
428	    .replace(/\buseFrame\b(?!s)/g, 'useCurrentFrame')
429	    .replace(/\buseConfig\b/g, 'useVideoConfig')
430	    .replace(/\bAbsoluteFills\b/g, 'AbsoluteFill')
431	    .replace(/\buseAudioReactives\b/g, 'useAudioReactive')
432	    .replace(/\binterpolates\b/g, 'interpolate');
433	
434	  validateExtractedEffectCode(code);
435	
436	  return {
437	    code,
438	    name,
439	    description,
440	    parameterSchema,
441	    message,
442	  };
443	}
444	
445	export function extractEffectCode(responseText: string): string {
446	  return extractEffectCodeAndMeta(responseText).code;
447	}
448	
449	const KNOWN_TYPOS: Array<[RegExp, string]> = [
450	  [/\buseCurrentFrames\b/, 'useCurrentFrames should be useCurrentFrame (no "s")'],
451	  [/\buseFrame\b(?!s)/, 'useFrame should be useCurrentFrame'],
452	  [/\buseConfig\b/, 'useConfig should be useVideoConfig'],
453	  [/\bAbsoluteFills\b/, 'AbsoluteFills should be AbsoluteFill (no "s")'],
454	  [/\buseAudioReactives\b/, 'useAudioReactives should be useAudioReactive (no trailing "s")'],
455	  [/\binterpolates\b/, 'interpolates should be interpolate (no "s")'],
456	];
457	
458	export function validateExtractedEffectCode(code: string): void {
459	  if (!code.trim()) {
460	    throw new Error('Effect generation returned empty code');
461	  }
462	
463	  if (!code.includes('exports.default')) {
464	    throw new Error('Generated effect code must assign the component with exports.default = ComponentName');
465	  }
466	
467	  if (/\bimport\s.+from\s/m.test(code) || /^\s*import\s/m.test(code)) {
468	    throw new Error('Generated effect code must not include import statements');
469	  }
470	
471	  if (/^\s*export\s/m.test(code)) {
472	    throw new Error('Generated effect code must not include export statements');
473	  }
474	
475	  if (!code.includes('React.createElement')) {
476	    throw new Error('Generated effect code must use React.createElement instead of JSX');
477	  }
478	
479	  // Math.random() creates non-deterministic renders — each frame capture gets
480	  // different values, causing flicker and inconsistent output.
481	  // Allow it only inside React.useMemo (for one-time IDs like SVG filter IDs).
482	  const codeWithoutMemos = code.replace(/React\.useMemo\([^)]*Math\.random\(\)[^)]*\)/g, '');
483	  if (/\bMath\.random\s*\(/.test(codeWithoutMemos)) {
484	    throw new Error('Generated effect must not use Math.random() — use deterministic math based on frame number instead');
485	  }
486	
487	  for (const [pattern, message] of KNOWN_TYPOS) {
488	    if (pattern.test(code)) {
489	      throw new Error(`Generated code contains a typo: ${message}`);
490	    }
491	  }
492	}
493

60	  const localFrame = frame % framesPerImage;
