1	import { useEffect, useMemo, useSyncExternalStore } from 'react';
2	import { DynamicEffectRegistry } from '@/tools/video-editor/effects/DynamicEffectRegistry';
3	import {
4	  continuousEffects,
5	  entranceEffects,
6	  exitEffects,
7	  getEffectRegistry,
8	  replaceEffectRegistry,
9	} from '@/tools/video-editor/effects';
10	import { loadDraftEffects } from '@/tools/video-editor/effects/effect-store';
11	import type { EffectResource } from '@/tools/video-editor/hooks/useEffectResources';
12	
13	const BUILT_INS = {
14	  ...entranceEffects,
15	  ...exitEffects,
16	  ...continuousEffects,
17	};
18	
19	/**
20	 * Dual-read registry: registers effects from both the legacy `effects` table
21	 * (keyed by slug) and the newer `resources` table (keyed by resource UUID).
22	 * Resource-based effects are stored in clips as `custom:{resourceId}`.
23	 */
24	export function useEffectRegistry(
25	  dbEffects: Array<{ slug: string; code: string }> | undefined,
26	  resourceEffects?: EffectResource[],
27	) {
28	  const draftEffects = useMemo(() => loadDraftEffects(), []);
29	  const registry = useMemo(() => new DynamicEffectRegistry(BUILT_INS), []);
30	  if (getEffectRegistry() !== registry) replaceEffectRegistry(registry);
31	  useSyncExternalStore(registry.subscribe, registry.getSnapshot);
32	
33	  useEffect(() => {
34	    let stale = false;
35	    const drafts = Object.entries(draftEffects);
36	    const db = dbEffects ?? [];
37	    const resources = resourceEffects ?? [];
38	    if (db.length > 0) {
39	      console.warn('[EffectRegistry] legacy DB effects are deprecated; migrate to resource-based effects via useEffectResources.');
40	    }
41	
42	    void registry.batch(async () => {
43	      for (const [name, code] of drafts) {
44	        if (stale) return;
45	        await registry.registerAsync(name, code);
46	      }
47	      for (const effect of db) {
48	        if (stale) return;
49	        await registry.registerAsync(effect.slug, effect.code);
50	      }
51	      for (const effect of resources) {
52	        if (stale) return;
53	        await registry.registerAsync(effect.id, effect.code, effect.parameterSchema);
54	      }
55	    });
56	
57	    return () => {
58	      stale = true;
59	    };
60	  }, [dbEffects, draftEffects, registry, resourceEffects]);
61	
62	  return registry;
63	}
64

Those prompts only become well-formed code if the request also carries `selected_clips` / `attached_clips` / `allowed_assets` / `allowed_asset_keys` (which the existing ai-generate-sequence already passes per sequenceGenerationService.ts:74-96) so the generated code can reference asset keys instead of inlining URLs. > AGENT

The StructuredOutput tool successfully delivered the flags. The reviewer found 7 concerns:

- **FLAG-001 (correctness, significant)**: TimelineRenderer dispatches via static const `SEQUENCE_COMPONENT_REGISTRY` lookup at lines 51-58/88; plan never specifies how it consults the dynamic registry or subscribes to async-compile updates. DB-stored sequences would render as the loud placeholder.
- **FLAG-002 (completeness, significant)**: `getClipCapabilityDescriptor`/`describeClipCapability` are static-const reads from CLIP_CAPABILITY_REGISTRY; the proposed `getResolvedSequenceClipCapabilityRegistry` runtime view has no path into the existing call sites in TimelineRenderer + capabilityManifest.
- **FLAG-003 (correctness, minor)**: Plan says "mirror useEffectRegistry via context" but useEffectRegistry uses a module-level singleton (`replaceEffectRegistry`), not React context. Plan's wording mixes both approaches.
- **FLAG-004 (completeness, significant)**: `ai-generate-sequence-component` body schema omits `selected_clips`/`allowed_assets`/`allowed_asset_keys`; the unified-UX classifier routes "use these images" prompts to it, but without asset keys the model can't write code that references user-selected images.
- **FLAG-005 (completeness, minor)**: `react-dom/server.renderToString` smoke render won't exercise Remotion or theme provider context; the success criterion overclaims relative to what the gate can detect.
- **FLAG-006 (completeness, minor)**: Fork-to-DB `getBundledComponentSource` mechanism is unspecified for Vite; LOCAL_SEQUENCE_REGISTRY entries lack the `<id>/{component.tsx,schema.json,defaults.json}` layout the plan implies.
- **FLAG-007 (maintainability, minor)**: Recurring debt — Step 10's registry wiring would replicate DEBT-047 (render-time mutation of module-state) on the sequences side.
